== Step 6b of 8: watch-folder mount (OPTIONAL - default is NO)
   Watch-Folder Autopilot can watch a folder on THIS computer: D:\Jarvis_Autopilot.
   For that, Docker needs a read-only link from that folder into the backend and
   worker containers, plus WATCH_FOLDER_PATH=/autopilot in your .env.
   Both files were backed up and validated in steps 4-5, so rollback undoes this too.
   If you answer NO (the default), nothing changes: Watch-Folder keeps watching its
   in-container default folder, and files dropped into D:\Jarvis_Autopilot do nothing.
Enable the D:\Jarvis_Autopilot watch-folder mount now? [y/N] -> N (default, answered automatically)
   Watch-folder mount NOT applied (you answered No). D:\Jarvis_Autopilot drops stay inert; the manual lines are in the installation guide if you change your mind.
